70:["$","ul",null,{"className":"x:[:is(ol,ul)_&]:my-[.75em] x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:list-disc x:ms-[1.5em]","children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Conflicts resolve per finding, not per edit."}]," The host groups a finding’s edits, considers the earliest group first, and accepts a group only when every member coexists with what it already accepted. 84:["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":["Two adapters, not one, ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"contributorAdapter"}]," for lint-class rules, ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"formatContributorAdapter"}]," for format-class. 85:["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Why two parallel interfaces?"}]," The engine’s internal ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"Rule"}]," is allowed to evolve freely; the public ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"rule.Rule"}]," is the stability boundary contributors compile against. 86:["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":[["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"registerContributors"}]," evaluates ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"Name"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"Visits"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"IsFormat"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"VisitsDeclarationFiles"}],", and the optional ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"OptionsRule"}]," capability once behind a recover barrier and stores the results in the adapter. ae:["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":["A trigger applies when the cursor sits inside ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"Scope"}]," and the line prefix contains ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"After"}],"; the text following the last occurrence of ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"After"}]," is what the editor filters on. ",["$","strong",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"After"}]," must end exactly where the completed token begins"]}]," — ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"\"@evidence \""}]," with its trailing space, not ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"\"@evidence\""}]," — or the token swallows the separator and nothing filters. When several triggers match one line, the occurrence nearest the cursor wins; at that occurrence the longest ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"After"}]," wins and only hints naming that same trigger are offered. That is what lets a corpus be layered — ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"\"@\""}],", ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"\"@evidence \""}],", and ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"\"@evidence docs/spec.md#\""}]," can all be published at once — while a later trigger, such as a second ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"\"@\""}]," further along the line, is never eclipsed by an earlier, longer one."]}]

b0:["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":"ttscserver fetches the corpus in the background and answers from memory. b1:["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":["The corpus is refetched after a saved document, a configuration change, or a watched-file change, so a rule enabled mid-session or an index rebuilt from a saved file reaches the editor without restarting the server.
